8a6(rdo rje gcod pa): gyis rab tu gang bar byas te/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la sbyin pa byin na/rab 'byor 'di ji snyam tu sems/skyes pa 'am bud med
8a7(rdo rje gcod pa): de gzhi de las bsod nams kyi mang du bskyed dam/rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das mang lagaso//bde bar gshegs pa mang lags te/skyes pa 'am bud med de gzhi de
8b1(rdo rje gcod pa): las bsod nams mang du bskyed do//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa/rab 'byor gang gis 'jig rten gyi khams de snyed rin po che sna bdun gyis rab tu gang bar byas te/de bzhin
8b2(rdo rje gcod pa): gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la sbyin pa byin pa bas/gang gis chos kyi rnam grangs 'di las tha ni tshig bzhi pa'i tshigasu bcad pa tsam gzung nas/zhan
8b3(rdo rje gcod pa): dag la yang bshad de yang dag par rab tu bstan na/de nyid de'i gzhi de las bsod nams ches mang du grangs med dpag tu med pa bskyed do//yang rab 'byor sa phyogs gang na chos kyi rnam grang+sa
8b4(rdo rje gcod pa): 'di las tha na tshig bzhi pa'i tshigasu bcadapa tsam 'don tam/gton pa'i sa phyogs de lha dang mi dang lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyi mchod rten du gyur pa na/su zhig chos kyi
8b5(rdo rje gcod pa): rnam grangs 'di len pa dang/'dzin pa dang/'chang ba dang/klog pa dang/kun chub par byed pa dang/tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa de ngo mtshar rab dang ldan par gyur pa lta ci smos/sa
8b6(rdo rje gcod pa): phyogs de na ston pa yang bzhugs te/bla ma'i gnas gzhan bdag kyang gnas so//de skad ces bka' bstsal pa dang/bcom lan 'das la tshe dang pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol
8b7(rdo rje gcod pa): to//bcom ldan 'das chos kyi rnam grangs 'di'i ming ci lags/ji ltar bzung bar bgyi/de skad ces gsol pa dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces bka'
9a1(rdo rje gcod pa): @#//bstsal to//rob 'byor chos kyi rnam grangs 'di shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa zhes bya ste/'di de ltar zung shig/de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor de bzhin gshes pas
9a2(rdo rje gcod pa): shes rab kyi pha rol du phyinapa gang gsungs pa de nyid pha rol du phyin pa med pa'i phyir te/des na shes rab kyi pha rol du phyi pa zhes bya'o//rab 'byor 'di ji snyam du sems/de bzhin
9a3(rdo rje gcod pa): gshegs pas gang gsungs pa'i chos de gang yang yod dam/rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pas gang gsungs pa chos de gang yang ma mchiso//bcom
9a4(rdo rje gcod pa): ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa/rab 'byor 'di ji snyam tu sems/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams na sa'i rdul phra rab ji snyed yod pa de mang ba yin nam/rab 'byor
9a5(rdo rje gcod pa): gyis gsol pa/bcom ldan das sa'i rdul de mang lagaso//bde bar gshegs pa mang lagaso//de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom ldan 'das sa'i rdul gang lags pa de rdul ma mchis
9a6(rdo rje gcod pa): par de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa'i slad du ste/des na sa'i rdul ces bgyi'o//'jig rten gyi khams gang lags de khams ma mchis par de bzhin gshegs pasagasungs te/des
9a7(rdo rje gcod pa): na 'jig rten gyi khams zhes bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsalapa/rab 'byor 'di ji snyamadu sems/skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis po de dag gis de bzhin
9b1(rdo rje gcod pa): gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas su lta'am/rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das de ni ma lagaso//de ci'i slad du zhe na/skyes bu chen po'i 
9b2(rdo rje gcod pa): mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis po gang dag de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa de dag mtshan ma mchis par de bzhin gshegs pas gsugs pa'i slad du ste/des na de bzhin gshegs 
9b3(rdo rje gcod pa): pa'i mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis zhes bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa/yang rab 'byor skyes pa 'am bud med gang gis lus gang ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed yongasu btang

9b6(rdo rje gcod pa): 'di de bzhin gshegs pas gsus pa ni bcom ldan 'das ngo mtshar lagaso//bde bar gshegs pa ngo mtshar lagaso//bcom ldan 'das bdag gis ye shes skyes tshun chad bdag
9b7(rdo rje gcod pa): gi chos kyi rnam grangs 'di sngon nams yang ma thos so//bcom ldan 'das kyis mdo bshad pa 'di la gang dag yang dag par 'du shes bskyed par 'gyur ba'i sems can de dag ni ngo 
10a1(rdo rje gcod pa): @#//mtshar rab dang ldan par 'gyur lagaso//de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom ldan 'das yang dag par 'du shes pa gang lags pa de nyid 'du shes ma mchis pa'i slad du ste/de
10a2(rdo rje gcod pa): bas na yang dag par 'du shes yang dag par 'du shes zhes de bzhin gshegs pa mi gsung ngo//bcom ldan 'das bdag chos kyi rnam grangs 'di bshad pa la rtog cing mos pa ni
10a3(rdo rje gcod pa): bdag ngo mtshar ba ma lags kyi/bcom ldan 'das slad ma'i tshe slad ma'i dus lnga brgya tha ma la seMn gang dag chos kyi rnam grangs 'dilen pa dang/'dzin pa dang/'chang ba dang/

10b4(rdo rje gcod pa): rab 'byor gang gi tshe ka ling ka'i rgyal pos nga'i yan lag dang nying lag rnam par gcad par gyur pa de'i tshe nga la bdag tu 'du shes sam/senaM du 'du shes sam/srog tu 'du shes saM/

10b7(rdo rje gcod pa): 'du shes kyang 'byung ba'i phyir ro//rab 'byor ngas mngon par shes te 'das pa'i dus na nga tshe rabs lnga brgyar bzod par smra ba zhes bya ba'i drang srong du gyur pa de na yang la bdag tu 'du
11a1(rdo rje gcod pa): @#//shes ma byung/sems can du 'du shes pa dang/srog tu 'du shes pa dang/gang zag tu 'du shes pa ma byung ngo//rab 'byor de lta bas na byang chub sems dpa' sems

11b4(rdo rje gcod pa): yang rab 'byor rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang dag chos kyi rnam grangs 'di len pa dang/'dzin pa dang/'chang ba dang/klog pa dang/kun chub par byed pa dang/gzhan dag la
